country_event = {
	id = cosmo_pirateaffairs_events.10
	title = cosmo_pirateaffairs_events.10.t
	desc = cosmo_pirateaffairs_events.10.d
	picture = cosmogfx_AFFAIR_NOBLEWOMAN_MENU_1_eventPicture
	
	hidden = yes
	
	trigger = {
		ai = no
	NOT = {
		capital_scope = {
			has_province_modifier = cosmo_pirateaffair_province
		}
	}	
		has_ruler_flag = cosmo_pirate_start
	}

	mean_time_to_happen = {
		days = 1
	}
	
		immediate = {
	hidden_effect = {
			capital_scope = {
				add_province_modifier = { 
					name = cosmo_pirateaffair_province
					duration = 365  
				} 	
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					any_province = {
						country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
						owner = {
						OR = {
							has_reform = cosmo_pirate_kingdom
							has_reform = pirate_republic_reform
						}
						}
						OR = {
							AND = {
								has_port = yes
								is_capital = yes
							}
							province_has_center_of_trade_of_level = 1
							#island?
						}
						NOT = {
							has_province_modifier = cosmo_pirates_allowed
						}
					}
				}
					random_province = {
						limit = {
						owner = {
						OR = {
							has_reform = cosmo_pirate_kingdom
							has_reform = pirate_republic_reform
						}
						}
						OR = {
							AND = {
								has_port = yes
								is_capital = yes
							}
							province_has_center_of_trade_of_level = 1
							#island?
						}
							NOT = {
								has_province_modifier = cosmo_pirates_allowed
							}							
						}
						add_province_modifier = { 
							name = cosmo_pirates_allowed
							desc = cosmo_pirates_allowed_DESC
							duration = -1  
						}
					}
			}
				random_list = {
				33 = { #adm
						set_ruler_flag = cosmo_pirate_advisor
				}
				66 = { #dip
						set_ruler_flag = cosmo_pirate_general
	
				}																								
			}
		if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					has_ruler_flag = had_cosmo_pirate_advisor	
					has_ruler_flag = cosmo_pirate_general	
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
						culture_group = british
						culture_group = french
						culture_group = african
						culture_group = iberian
						culture_group = gaelic

# Onsen Gemeral Asian
country_event = {
	id = cosmo_pirateaffairs_events.200
	title = cosmo_pirateaffairs_events.200.t
	desc = cosmo_pirateaffairs_events.200.d
	picture = {
	trigger = {
		has_ruler_flag = cosmo_pirate_1	
			}	
	picture = cosmogfx_PIRATE_GENERAL_1_eventPicture
	}
	picture = {
	trigger = {
		has_ruler_flag = cosmo_pirate_2	
			}	
	picture = cosmogfx_PIRATE_GENERAL_2_eventPicture
	}
	picture = {
	trigger = {
		has_ruler_flag = cosmo_pirate_3	
			}	
	picture = cosmogfx_PIRATE_GENERAL_3_eventPicture
	}
	picture = {
	trigger = {
		has_ruler_flag = cosmo_pirate_4	
			}	
	picture = cosmogfx_PIRATE_GENERAL_4_eventPicture
	}
	picture = {
	trigger = {
		has_ruler_flag = cosmo_pirate_5	
			}	
	picture = cosmogfx_PIRATE_GENERAL_5_eventPicture
	}
	picture = {
	trigger = {
		has_ruler_flag = cosmo_pirate_6	
			}	
	picture = cosmogfx_PIRATE_GENERAL_6_eventPicture
	}
	picture = {
	trigger = {
		has_ruler_flag = cosmo_pirate_7	
			}	
	picture = cosmogfx_PIRATE_GENERAL_7_eventPicture
	}
	picture = {
	trigger = {
		has_ruler_flag = cosmo_pirate_8	
			}	
	picture = cosmogfx_PIRATE_GENERAL_8_eventPicture
	}
	picture = {
	trigger = {
		has_ruler_flag = cosmo_pirate_9	
			}	
	picture = cosmogfx_PIRATE_GENERAL_9_eventPicture
	}		
	
	
	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		ai = no
	}

	
		immediate = {
	hidden_effect = {	
		random_province = {
			limit = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				has_building = port_of_pleasure
				has_province_modifier = cosmo_pirates_allowed				
			}
			save_event_target_as = popprovince
		}
		random_province = {
			limit = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				has_building = port_of_pleasure
				has_province_modifier = cosmo_pirates_allowed
			OR = {
				province_id = 487 #Port Royal 
				province_id = 482 #New Providence
				province_id = 489 # Tortuga
				province_id = 4024 #Libertatia

			}
			}
			save_event_target_as = popprovince
		}		
				random_list = {
				33 = { #adm
						set_ruler_flag = pirate_adm
				}
				33 = { #dip
						set_ruler_flag = pirate_dip
	
				}
				33 = { #mil
						set_ruler_flag = pirate_mil
				}																								
			}		
		}
		}

	
		option = {
		name = cosmo_pirateaffairs_events.200.a
		trigger_switch = {
			on_trigger = has_ruler_flag
			pirate_adm = {
					if = {
						limit = {
						NOT = {
							has_ruler_flag = has_affair_adm
						}	
						}
						change_adm = 1
						set_ruler_flag = has_affair_adm
						}
						if = {
							limit = {
								has_ruler_flag = has_affair_adm
							}
							capital_scope = {

								add_scaled_local_adm_power = 2 	
										
							}							
						}				
					}
			pirate_dip = {
						if = {
							limit = {
							NOT = {
								has_ruler_flag = has_affair_dip
							}	
							}
							change_dip = 1
							set_ruler_flag = has_affair_dip
							}
							if = {
								limit = {
									has_ruler_flag = has_affair_dip
								}
							capital_scope = {

									add_scaled_local_dip_power = 2 	
								}
							}				
						}
			pirate_mil = {
						if = {
							limit = {
							NOT = {
								has_ruler_flag = has_affair_mil
							}	
							}
							change_mil = 1
							set_ruler_flag = has_affair_mil
							}
							if = {
								limit = {
									has_ruler_flag = has_affair_mil
								}
							capital_scope = {

									add_scaled_local_mil_power = 2 	
								}
							}				
						}										
				}
		clr_ruler_flag = cosmo_pirate_1	
		clr_ruler_flag = cosmo_pirate_2	
		clr_ruler_flag = cosmo_pirate_3	
		clr_ruler_flag = cosmo_pirate_4	
		clr_ruler_flag = cosmo_pirate_5	
		clr_ruler_flag = cosmo_pirate_6	
		clr_ruler_flag = cosmo_pirate_7	
		clr_ruler_flag = cosmo_pirate_8	
		clr_ruler_flag = cosmo_pirate_9
		clr_ruler_flag = pirate_adm																
		clr_ruler_flag = pirate_dip																
		clr_ruler_flag = pirate_mil																
		}
		option = {
		name = cosmo_pirateaffairs_events.200.b
		clr_ruler_flag = cosmo_pirate_start
		trigger_switch = {
			on_trigger = has_ruler_flag
			pirate_adm = {
						change_adm = -1				
					}
			pirate_dip = {
						change_dip = -1
						}
			pirate_mil = {
						change_mil = -1
				
						}										
				}
		clr_ruler_flag = cosmo_pirate_1	
		clr_ruler_flag = cosmo_pirate_2	
		clr_ruler_flag = cosmo_pirate_3	
		clr_ruler_flag = cosmo_pirate_4	
		clr_ruler_flag = cosmo_pirate_5	
		clr_ruler_flag = cosmo_pirate_6	
		clr_ruler_flag = cosmo_pirate_7	
		clr_ruler_flag = cosmo_pirate_8	
		clr_ruler_flag = cosmo_pirate_9
		clr_ruler_flag = pirate_adm																
		clr_ruler_flag = pirate_dip																
		clr_ruler_flag = pirate_mil																
	}					
}

	}

			}	
	picture = cosmogfx_PIRATE_GENERAL_59_eventPicture
	}		
	
	
	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		ai = no
	}

	
		immediate = {
	hidden_effect = {	
		random_province = {
			limit = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				has_building = port_of_pleasure
				has_province_modifier = cosmo_pirates_allowed				
			}
			save_event_target_as = popprovince
		}
		random_province = {
			limit = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				has_building = port_of_pleasure
				has_province_modifier = cosmo_pirates_allowed
			OR = {
				province_id = 487 #Port Royal 
				province_id = 482 #New Providence
				province_id = 489 # Tortuga
				province_id = 4024 #Libertatia

			}
			}
			save_event_target_as = popprovince
		}		
				random_list = {
				33 = { #adm
						set_ruler_flag = pirate_adm
				}
				33 = { #dip
						set_ruler_flag = pirate_dip
	
				}
				33 = { #mil
						set_ruler_flag = pirate_mil
				}																								
			}		
		}
		}

	
		option = {
		name = cosmo_pirateaffairs_events.205.a
		trigger_switch = {
			on_trigger = has_ruler_flag
			pirate_adm = {
					if = {
						limit = {
						NOT = {
							has_ruler_flag = has_affair_adm
						}	
						}
						change_adm = 1
						set_ruler_flag = has_affair_adm
						}
						if = {
							limit = {
								has_ruler_flag = has_affair_adm
							}
							capital_scope = {

								add_scaled_local_adm_power = 2 	
										
							}							
						}				
					}
			pirate_dip = {
						if = {
							limit = {
							NOT = {
								has_ruler_flag = has_affair_dip
							}	
							}
							change_dip = 1
							set_ruler_flag = has_affair_dip
							}
							if = {
								limit = {
									has_ruler_flag = has_affair_dip
								}
							capital_scope = {

									add_scaled_local_dip_power = 2 	
								}
							}				
						}
			pirate_mil = {
						if = {
							limit = {
							NOT = {
								has_ruler_flag = has_affair_mil
							}	
							}
							change_mil = 1
							set_ruler_flag = has_affair_mil
							}
							if = {
								limit = {
									has_ruler_flag = has_affair_mil
								}
							capital_scope = {

									add_scaled_local_mil_power = 2 	
								}
							}				
						}										
				}
		clr_ruler_flag = cosmo_pirate_50	
		clr_ruler_flag = cosmo_pirate_51	
		clr_ruler_flag = cosmo_pirate_52	
		clr_ruler_flag = cosmo_pirate_53	
		clr_ruler_flag = cosmo_pirate_54	
		clr_ruler_flag = cosmo_pirate_55	
		clr_ruler_flag = cosmo_pirate_56	
		clr_ruler_flag = cosmo_pirate_57	
		clr_ruler_flag = cosmo_pirate_58
		clr_ruler_flag = cosmo_pirate_59
		clr_ruler_flag = pirate_adm

		has_ruler_flag = cosmo_pirate_89	
			}	
	picture = cosmogfx_PIRATE_GENERAL_89_eventPicture
	}		
	
	
	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		ai = no
	}

	
		immediate = {
	hidden_effect = {	
		random_province = {
			limit = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				has_building = port_of_pleasure
				has_province_modifier = cosmo_pirates_allowed				
			}
			save_event_target_as = popprovince
		}
		random_province = {
			limit = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				has_building = port_of_pleasure
				has_province_modifier = cosmo_pirates_allowed
			OR = {
				province_id = 487 #Port Royal 
				province_id = 482 #New Providence
				province_id = 489 # Tortuga
				province_id = 4024 #Libertatia

			}
			}
			save_event_target_as = popprovince
		}		
				random_list = {
				33 = { #adm
						set_ruler_flag = pirate_adm
				}
				33 = { #dip
						set_ruler_flag = pirate_dip
	
				}
				33 = { #mil
						set_ruler_flag = pirate_mil
				}																								
			}		
		}
		}

	
		option = {
		name = cosmo_pirateaffairs_events.208.a
		trigger_switch = {
			on_trigger = has_ruler_flag
			pirate_adm = {
					if = {
						limit = {
						NOT = {
							has_ruler_flag = has_affair_adm
						}	
						}
						change_adm = 1
						set_ruler_flag = has_affair_adm
						}
						if = {
							limit = {
								has_ruler_flag = has_affair_adm
							}
							capital_scope = {

								add_scaled_local_adm_power = 2 	
										
							}							
						}				
					}
			pirate_dip = {
						if = {
							limit = {
							NOT = {
								has_ruler_flag = has_affair_dip
							}	
							}
							change_dip = 1
							set_ruler_flag = has_affair_dip
							}
							if = {
								limit = {
									has_ruler_flag = has_affair_dip
								}
							capital_scope = {

									add_scaled_local_dip_power = 2 	
								}
							}				
						}
			pirate_mil = {
						if = {
							limit = {
							NOT = {
								has_ruler_flag = has_affair_mil
							}	
							}
							change_mil = 1
							set_ruler_flag = has_affair_mil
							}
							if = {
								limit = {
									has_ruler_flag = has_affair_mil
								}
							capital_scope = {

									add_scaled_local_mil_power = 2 	
								}
							}				
						}										
				}
		clr_ruler_flag = cosmo_pirate_80	
		clr_ruler_flag = cosmo_pirate_81	
		clr_ruler_flag = cosmo_pirate_82	
		clr_ruler_flag = cosmo_pirate_83	
		clr_ruler_flag = cosmo_pirate_84	
		clr_ruler_flag = cosmo_pirate_85	
		clr_ruler_flag = cosmo_pirate_86	
		clr_ruler_flag = cosmo_pirate_87	
		clr_ruler_flag = cosmo_pirate_88
		clr_ruler_flag = cosmo_pirate_89
		clr_ruler_flag = pirate_adm																
		clr_ruler_flag = pirate_dip																
		clr_ruler_flag = pirate_mil																
		}	
		option = {
		name = cosmo_pirateaffairs_events.208.b
		clr_ruler_flag = cosmo_pirate_start
		trigger_switch = {
			on_trigger = has_ruler_flag
			pirate_adm = {
						change_adm = -1				
					}
			pirate_dip = {
						change_dip = -1
						}
			pirate_mil = {
						change_mil = -1
				
						}										
				}
		clr_ruler_flag = cosmo_pirate_80	
		clr_ruler_flag = cosmo_pirate_81	
		clr_ruler_flag = cosmo_pirate_82	
		clr_ruler_flag = cosmo_pirate_83

		clr_ruler_flag = cosmo_pirate_85

				has_province_modifier = cosmo_pirates_allowed				
			}
			save_event_target_as = popprovince
		}

			limit = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				has_building = port_of_pleasure
				has_province_modifier = cosmo_pirates_allowed
			OR = {
				province_id = 487 #Port Royal 
				province_id = 482 #New Providence
				province_id = 489 # Tortuga
				province_id = 4024 #Libertatia

			}
			}
			save_event_target_as = popprovince
		}		
				random_list = {
				33 = { #adm
						set_ruler_flag = pirate_adm
				}
				33 = { #dip
						set_ruler_flag = pirate_dip
	
				}
				33 = { #mil
						set_ruler_flag = pirate_mil
				}																								
			}		
		}
		}

	
		option = {
		name = cosmo_pirateaffairs_events.212.a
		trigger_switch = {
			on_trigger = has_ruler_flag
			pirate_adm = {
					if = {
						limit = {
						NOT = {
							has_ruler_flag = has_affair_adm
						}	
						}
						change_adm = 1
						set_ruler_flag = has_affair_adm
						}
						if = {
							limit = {
								has_ruler_flag = has_affair_adm
							}
							capital_scope = {

								add_scaled_local_adm_power = 2 	
										
							}							
						}				
					}
			pirate_dip = {
						if = {
							limit = {
							NOT = {
								has_ruler_flag = has_affair_dip
							}	
							}
							change_dip = 1
							set_ruler_flag = has_affair_dip
							}
							if = {
								limit = {
									has_ruler_flag = has_affair_dip
								}

							}

							}	
							}
							change_mil = 1
							set_ruler_flag = has_affair_mil
							}
							if = {
								limit = {
									has_ruler_flag = has_affair_mil
								}
							capital_scope = {

									add_scaled_local_mil_power = 2 	
								}
							}				
						}										
				}
		clr_ruler_flag = cosmo_asian_pirate_1	
		clr_ruler_flag = cosmo_asian_pirate_2	
		clr_ruler_flag = cosmo_asian_pirate_3	
		clr_ruler_flag = cosmo_asian_pirate_4

	}

	}

	
		immediate = {
	hidden_effect = {	
		random_province = {
			limit = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				has_building = port_of_pleasure
				has_province_modifier = cosmo_pirates_allowed				
			}
			save_event_target_as = popprovince
		}
		random_province = {
			limit = {
				country_or_vassal_holds = ROOT
				has_building = port_of_pleasure
				has_province_modifier = cosmo_pirates_allowed
			OR = {
				province_id = 487 #Port Royal 
				province_id = 482 #New Providence
				province_id = 489 # Tortuga
				province_id = 4024 #Libertatia

			}
			}
			save_event_target_as = popprovince
		}		
				random_list = {
				33 = { #adm
						set_ruler_flag = pirate_adm
				}
				33 = { #dip
						set_ruler_flag = pirate_dip
	
				}
				33 = { #mil
						set_ruler_flag = pirate_mil
				}																								
			}		
		}
		}

	
		option = {
		name = cosmo_pirateaffairs_events.216.a
		trigger_switch = {
			on_trigger = has_ruler_flag
			pirate_adm = {
					if = {
						limit = {
						NOT = {
							has_ruler_flag = has_affair_adm
						}	
						}
						change_adm = 1
						set_ruler_flag = has_affair_adm
						}
						if = {
							limit = {
								has_ruler_flag = has_affair_adm
							}
							capital_scope = {

								add_scaled_local_adm_power = 2 	
										
							}							
						}				
					}
			pirate_dip = {
						if = {
							limit = {
							NOT = {
								has_ruler_flag = has_affair_dip
							}	
							}
							change_dip = 1
							set_ruler_flag = has_affair_dip
							}
							if = {
								limit = {
									has_ruler_flag = has_affair_dip
								}
							capital_scope = {

									add_scaled_local_dip_power = 2 	
								}
							}				
						}
			pirate_mil = {
						if = {
							limit = {
							NOT = {
								has_ruler_flag = has_affair_mil
							}	
							}
							change_mil = 1
							set_ruler_flag = has_affair_mil
							}
							if = {
								limit = {
									has_ruler_flag = has_affair_mil
								}
							capital_scope = {

									add_scaled_local_mil_power = 2 	
								}
							}				
						}										
				}
		clr_ruler_flag = cosmo_african_pirate_10	
		clr_ruler_flag = cosmo_african_pirate_11	
		clr_ruler_flag = cosmo_african_pirate_12	
		clr_ruler_flag = cosmo_african_pirate_13	
		clr_ruler_flag = cosmo_african_pirate_14	
		clr_ruler_flag = cosmo_african_pirate_15	
		clr_ruler_flag = cosmo_african_pirate_16	
		clr_ruler_flag = cosmo_african_pirate_17	
		clr_ruler_flag = cosmo_african_pirate_18
		clr_ruler_flag = cosmo_african_pirate_19
		clr_ruler_flag = pirate_adm																
		clr_ruler_flag = pirate_dip																
		clr_ruler_flag = pirate_mil																
		}
		option = {
		name = cosmo_pirateaffairs_events.216.b
		clr_ruler_flag = cosmo_african_pirate_start
		trigger_switch = {
			on_trigger = has_ruler_flag
			pirate_adm = {
						change_adm = -1				
					}
			pirate_dip = {
						change_dip = -1
						}
			pirate_mil = {

						}

		option = {
		name = cosmo_pirateaffairs_events.306.b
		clr_ruler_flag = cosmo_pirate_start
		trigger_switch = {
			on_trigger = has_ruler_flag
			pirate_adm = {
						change_adm = -1
